, careful calculations yield

\displaystyle(\mathrm{EQ})=\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\Big({\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t_{n-1}))\Big)-b_{h}^{L}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n},\{\rho_{p}^{n},\rho_{\lambda}^{n}\})

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\Big({\mathbf{m}}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{n-1},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t_{n-1}))\Big)-{\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})-{\mathbf{g}}_{h}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})

\displaystyle+b_{h}^{L}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n},\{p^{n},\lambda^{n}\})-b^{L}((\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell},\{p^{n},\lambda^{n}\})+c_{h}^{(\cdot)}(\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1};{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h})-c^{(\cdot)}({\mathbf{u}}^{n};{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})+{\mathbf{m}}({\bm{f}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}({\bm{f}}_{h}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})

\displaystyle+a_{S,h}(\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-a_{S}({\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})-c_{h}^{(\cdot)}(\underline{{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-c_{h}^{(\cdot)}(\underline{\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}}(t_{n});{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})

\displaystyle=:\text{Err}_{1}^{I}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})+\text{Err}_{2}^{I}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})+\text{Err}_{1}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})+\text{Err}_{2}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})+\text{Err}_{3}^{C,(\cdot)}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})+\text{Err}_{4}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})+\text{Err}_{5}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})+\mathcal{C}^{(\cdot)}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}),

where we have used

c_{h}^{(\cdot)}(\underline{\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}}(t_{n});\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h})-c_{h}^{(\cdot)}(\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h})=-c_{h}^{(\cdot)}(\underline{{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-c_{h}^{(\cdot)}(\underline{\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}}(t_{n});{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}).

The second equation in (
EQ
) follows since
\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n,div}
for all
n\geq 0
;
\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{0}=\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}
see
Remark

7.1
.
∎

Lemma 7.2

(Interpolation errors)
.

The following interpolation error holds for all
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n}

(7.7)

\displaystyle|\textsc{Err}^{I}_{1}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{c}{\sqrt{\Delta t}}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}\sum_{j=0}^{1}||(\partial^{\circ}_{h})^{j}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}(t)||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

(7.8)

\displaystyle|\textsc{Err}^{I}_{2}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|

\displaystyle\leq c(h^{k_{pr}+1}+h^{k_{\lambda}+1})(||p^{n}||_{H^{k_{pr}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}+||\lambda^{n}||_{H^{k_{\lambda}+1}(\Gamma^{n})})||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}},

If furthermore
\underline{k_{\lambda}=k_{u}}
and
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n,div}
the following holds

(7.9)

\displaystyle|\textsc{Err}^{I}_{1}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|\leq c(\dfrac{h^{\widehat{r}_{u}}+h^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1}}{\sqrt{\Delta t}}+h^{\widehat{r}_{u}}\sqrt{\Delta t})\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}\sum_{j=0}^{1}||(\partial^{\circ})^{j}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}},

where
\widehat{r}_{u}=min\{k_{u},k_{g}\}
and the constants
c>0
are independent of
h
and
t
.

Proof.

Rewriting
\textsc{Err}^{I}_{1}(\cdot)
in integral form and using the transport property (
4.40
), the time-inequality (
6.13
), the Ritz-Stokes projection estimates (
5.11
), (
5.42
) and a simple Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, leads to

(7.10)

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\Big({\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t_{n-1}))\Big)=\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}\frac{d}{dt}\Big({\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}(t),\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))\Big)\,dt

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}(t),\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))+{\mathbf{g}}_{h}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}(t),\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))\,dt

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{c}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}(||\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}+||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))})||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\,dt

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{c}{\sqrt{\Delta t}}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}(||\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}^{2}+||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}^{2})\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

yielding the desired result. Next, (
7.8
) can be dealt with through standard techniques using the
b_{h}^{L}
bound (
4.62
) and the interpolation errors (
7.4
).

Now regarding the estimate (
7.9
), assuming
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n,div}
, going back to the second line of (
7.10
) and splitting the first term into a tangent and a normal direction, along with the Leray time-projection (
6.17
) from time
t^{n}
to time
t
for some
t\in[t_{n-1},t_{n}]
, i.e.
\hat{{\mathbf{v}}}_{h}^{n}(t)\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{t,div}
, we see that

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}(t),\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))=\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}{\mathbf{m}}_{h}({\mathbf{P}}\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}(t),\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))+{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}(t)\cdot{\mathbf{n}}(t),\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t)\cdot{\mathbf{n}}(t))

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}{\mathbf{m}}_{h}({\mathbf{P}}\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}(t),\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))+{\mathbf{m}}_{h}\big(\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}(t)\cdot{\mathbf{n}}(t),(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t)-\hat{{\mathbf{v}}}_{h}^{n}(t))\cdot{\mathbf{n}}(t)+\hat{{\mathbf{v}}}_{h}^{n}(t)\cdot{\mathbf{n}}(t)\big)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{\Delta t}}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||{\mathbf{P}}\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

(7.11)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{1}{{\Delta t}}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}\cdot{\mathbf{n}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}^{2}\big(c||(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t)-\hat{{\mathbf{v}}}_{h}^{n}(t))||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}+||\hat{{\mathbf{v}}}_{h}^{n}(t)\cdot{\mathbf{n}}(t)||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}\big)\,dt.

Using the Leray time-projection estimates (
6.20
) and (
6.18
) (they still hold for
\hat{{\mathbf{v}}}_{h}^{n}(t)\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{t,div}
) along with the normal control (
4.68
) and geometric estimate (
4.15
) we have

(7.12)

\displaystyle||(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t)-\hat{{\mathbf{v}}}_{h}^{n}(t))||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

\displaystyle\leq\Delta t||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}},

\displaystyle||\hat{{\mathbf{v}}}_{h}^{n}(t)\cdot{\mathbf{n}}(t)||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}

\displaystyle\leq||\hat{{\mathbf{v}}}_{h}^{n}(t)\cdot({\mathbf{n}}(t)-{\mathbf{n}}_{h}(t))||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}+||\hat{{\mathbf{v}}}_{h}^{n}(t)\cdot{\mathbf{n}}_{h}(t))||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}

(7.13)

\displaystyle\leq ch||\hat{{\mathbf{v}}}_{h}^{n}(t)||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}\leq ch||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}.

Therefore, plugging (
7.12
) and (
7.1
) into (
7.1
) and using the Ritz-Stokes estimate (
5.42
) we find

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}(t),\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))\leq\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{\Delta t}}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||{\mathbf{P}}\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}^{2}+h||\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}\cdot{\mathbf{n}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\Delta t}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}_{h}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}\cdot{\mathbf{n}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}}

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{h^{\widehat{r}_{u}}+h^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1}}{\sqrt{\Delta t}}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}\sum_{j=0}^{1}||(\partial^{\circ})^{j}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

\displaystyle+h^{\widehat{r}_{u}}\sqrt{\Delta t}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}\sum_{j=0}^{1}||(\partial^{\circ})^{j}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}},

where we also applied the norm equivalence (
4.31
) and (
4.36
) as needed. This gives our assertion.
∎

Lemma 7.3

(Consistency errors)
.

There exists a constant
c>0
independent of
h
and
t
such that for all
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n}
the following error bounds hold

\displaystyle|\textsc{Err}_{1}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|\leq c\sqrt{\Delta t}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\,+\frac{ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1}}{\sqrt{\Delta t}}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}+||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

(7.14)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\,+ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sup_{t\in[t_{n-1},t_{n}]}||{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+\big(||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\big)||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

(7.15)

\displaystyle|\textsc{Err}_{2}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|\leq ch^{k_{g}}(||p^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}+||\lambda^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{n})})||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

(7.16)

\displaystyle|\textsc{Err}_{3}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|\leq c\big(\sqrt{\Delta t}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}\!\!\!\!\!+h^{k_{g}}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n-1})}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}\big)||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

\displaystyle|\textsc{Err}_{3}^{C,cov}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|\leq c\big(\sqrt{\Delta t}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}}

(7.17)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+h^{k_{g}}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n-1})}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}\big)||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

(7.18)

\displaystyle|\textsc{Err}_{4}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|\leq ch^{k_{g}+1}||{\bm{f}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}.

Proof.

Let us estimate each consistency error separately.

\textsc{Err}_{1}^{C}:
We rewrite our expression in integral form, apply the transport formula (
4.40
) to find that

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\textsc{Err}_{1}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h})=\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\partial^{\circ}_{h}{\mathbf{u}},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))+{\mathbf{g}}_{h}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};{\mathbf{u}},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))\,dt-{\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})-{\mathbf{g}}_{h}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})

(7.19)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!=\underbrace{\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}\big[{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\partial^{\circ}_{h}{\mathbf{u}},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))-{\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}_{\ell}{\mathbf{u}},(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))^{\ell})+{\mathbf{g}}_{h}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};{\mathbf{u}},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))-{\mathbf{g}}({\mathbf{v}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};{\mathbf{u}},(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))^{\ell})\big]\,dt}_{=:\mathcal{E}_{1}}

\displaystyle\!\!+\underbrace{\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}\big[{\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}_{\ell}{\mathbf{u}},(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))^{\ell})+{\mathbf{g}}({\mathbf{v}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};{\mathbf{u}},(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))^{\ell})\big]\,dt-{\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})-{\mathbf{g}}_{h}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}),}_{=:\mathcal{E}_{2}}

where using the geometric perturbations (
4.71
), (
4.78
), the time inequality (
6.13
), (
4.36
), the norm equivalence (
4.31
) and a Cauchy-Schwartz inequality as in (
7.10
) we obtain

\displaystyle|\mathcal{E}_{1}|\leq\frac{ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1}}{\sqrt{\Delta t}}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}.

Now for
\mathcal{E}_{2}
, first recall the transport formulae (
3.6
) and (
4.45
) to see that

(7.20)

{\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}_{\ell}{\mathbf{u}},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})+{\mathbf{g}}({\mathbf{v}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};{\mathbf{u}},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})={\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})+{\mathbf{g}}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma}^{N};{\mathbf{u}},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})-{\mathbf{m}}({\mathbf{u}},(\partial^{\circ}_{\ell}-\partial^{\circ})\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell}),

therefore we readily see that

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}\Big[{\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}},(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))^{\ell})-{\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})+{\mathbf{g}}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma}^{N};{\mathbf{u}},(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))^{\ell})-{\mathbf{g}}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};{\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})

\displaystyle+{\mathbf{m}}({\mathbf{u}},(\partial^{\circ}_{\ell}-\partial^{\circ})(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))^{\ell})\Big]\,dt+\Big({\mathbf{g}}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma}^{N};{\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})-{\mathbf{g}}_{h}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})\Big)=:\mathcal{E}_{2}^{1}+\mathcal{E}_{2}^{2}.

Once again rewriting the expressions in integral form, along with time inequality (
6.13
), the bound (
4.36
), transport formulae in
Lemma

3.1
and the velocity
{\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma}^{N}
assumptions (
2.15
), we obtain

\displaystyle|\mathcal{E}_{2}^{1}|\leq\Big|\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}\int_{t}^{t^{n}}\frac{d}{ds}\big({\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}},(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(s))^{\ell})+{\mathbf{g}}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma}^{N};{\mathbf{u}},(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(s))^{\ell})\big)\,ds\,dt+\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}{\mathbf{m}}({\mathbf{u}},(\partial^{\circ}_{\ell}-\partial^{\circ})(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t))^{\ell})\,dt\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}\int_{t}^{t^{n}}{\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}},(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(s))^{\ell})+2{\mathbf{g}}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma}^{N};\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}},(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(s))^{\ell})+{\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}\textnormal{div}_{\Gamma}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma}^{N})\,\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}},(\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(s))^{\ell})\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle+ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1}\!\!\!\!\!\!\sup_{t\in[t_{n-1},t_{n}]}\big(||{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}\big)\,||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

\displaystyle\leq c\sqrt{\Delta t}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sup_{t\in[t_{n-1},t_{n}]}\big(||{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}\big)||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}.

Adding and subtract suitable terms we also see from (
4.35
), (
4.78
), and the bound (
4.59
) that

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{g}}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma}^{N}-{\mathbf{v}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};{\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})-\mathrm{G}_{{\mathbf{g}}}({\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{g}}_{h}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{g}}_{h}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})

\displaystyle\leq ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+\big(||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\big)||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}.

Combining the bounds of
\mathcal{E}_{1}
and
\mathcal{E}_{2}
into (
7.1
) we obtain (
7.3
).

\underline{\textsc{Err}^{C}_{2}},\underline{\textsc{Err}^{C}_{2}}:
These estimates are straightforward if we simply apply (
4.16
) and (
4.71
) respectively.

\underline{\textsc{Err}^{C}_{3}}:
Adding and subtracting appropriate terms we see that

(7.21)

\textsc{Err}^{C}_{3}=c_{h}(\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h})-c(\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})+c(\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})-c({\mathbf{u}}^{n};{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})=:\mathrm{C}_{1}+\mathrm{C}_{2}.

First recall
c(\bullet;\bullet,\bullet)
defined in (
3.13
) (with
\eta_{1},\eta_{2}=0
). Then, we see that using the perturbations in
Lemma

4.14
, the geometric estimates
||{\mathbf{P}}-{\mathbf{P}_{h}}||_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}\leq ch^{k_{g}}
,
||1-\mu_{h}||_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{h}(t))}\leq ch^{k_{g}+1}
as seen in
Section

4.3
, (
4.17
), and the time inequalities (
6.13
), (
7.5
) we obtain

(7.22)

\displaystyle|\mathrm{C}_{1}|

\displaystyle=\Big|\frac{1}{2}\Big(\int_{\Gamma_{h}^{n}}((\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n})\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma_{h}}){\mathbf{u}}^{n})\cdot\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}-\int_{\Gamma^{n}}((\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n})\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}){\mathbf{u}}^{n})\cdot(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\frac{1}{2}\Big(\int_{\Gamma_{h}^{n}}((\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n})\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma_{h}})\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\int_{\Gamma^{n}}((\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n})\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma})(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq ch^{k_{g}}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n-1})}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}.

For the second estimate in (
7.21
) we have that

\displaystyle|\mathrm{C}_{2}|=\frac{1}{2}\Big|\int_{\Gamma^{n}}\big(({\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n}))\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\big)\cdot(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell}\Big|+\frac{1}{2}\Big|\int_{\Gamma^{n}}\big(({\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n}))\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}\big)(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\Big|.

From the fact that
{\mathbf{u}}^{n}=\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}(t_{n})={\mathbf{u}}(t_{n})\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{t_{n}}\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{-t_{n}}
, and
\dfrac{d}{dt}{\mathbf{u}}(t)\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{t}=\big(\dfrac{d}{dt}{\mathbf{u}}(t)+(\nabla{\mathbf{u}}){\mathbf{v}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N}\big)\Phi^{\ell}_{t}=\partial^{\circ}_{\ell}{\mathbf{u}}(t)\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{t}
(see (
4.33
)) it follows that

(7.23)

\displaystyle{\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n})

\displaystyle={\mathbf{u}}(t_{n})\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{t_{n}}\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{-t_{n}}-{\mathbf{u}}(t_{n-1})\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{t_{n-1}}\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{-t_{n}}

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t_{n}}\frac{d}{dt}{\mathbf{u}}(s)\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{s}\,ds\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{-t_{n}}=\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t_{n}}\partial^{\circ}_{\ell}{\mathbf{u}}\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{s}\,ds\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{-t_{n}},

and therefore a simple use of Hölder’s inequality, the Sobolev embedding
L^{4}\hookrightarrow H^{1}
, (
4.36
) and, by this point, standard use of the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, shows that

(7.24)

\displaystyle|\mathrm{C}_{2}|\leq c\sqrt{\Delta t}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t_{n}}||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}}||{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\Big)^{1/2}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

in which we also used the norm equivalence (
4.31
) where appropriate. Therefore inserting (
7.22
), and (
7.24
) into (
7.21
) gives our assertion (
7.16
).

\underline{\textsc{Err}^{C,cov}_{3}}:
As above, adding and subtracting appropriate terms we see that

\displaystyle\textsc{Err}^{C,cov}_{3}

\displaystyle=c_{h}^{cov}(\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h})-c^{cov}(\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})+c^{cov}(\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})-c^{cov}({\mathbf{u}}^{n};{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})

(7.25)

\displaystyle=:\mathrm{C}_{1}^{cov}+\mathrm{C}_{2}^{cov},

where we recall
c_{h}^{cov}
,
c^{cov}
as defined in
Definition

4.1
and (
3.13
) (with
\eta_{1},\eta_{2}=0
) respectively. Now following (
7.21
) and using the fact that
\nabla_{\Gamma}^{cov}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell}=\nabla_{\Gamma}^{cov}({\mathbf{P}_{\Gamma}}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})+(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell}\cdot{\mathbf{n}})\bm{H}
(
2.8
), we clearly see that

\displaystyle|\mathrm{C}_{1}^{cov}|\leq ch^{k_{g}}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n-1})}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+\Big|-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{h}^{n}}{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\cdot{\mathbf{n}}_{h}\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n})\cdot\bm{H}_{h}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\Big|

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!+\Big|\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{h}^{n}}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\cdot({\mathbf{n}}_{h}-{\mathbf{n}})\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n})\cdot\bm{H}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\Big|+\Big|-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{h}^{n}}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\cdot{\mathbf{n}})\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n})\cdot\bm{H}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma^{n}}((\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell}\cdot{\mathbf{n}_{\Gamma}})\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n})\cdot\bm{H}{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\Big|

(7.26)

\displaystyle\leq ch^{k_{g}}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n-1})}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

where we used the fact that
{\mathbf{u}}^{n}
solves (
eNSWc
) and therefore
{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\cdot{\mathbf{n}}_{h}={\mathbf{u}}^{n}\cdot({\mathbf{n}}_{h}-{\mathbf{n}})
coupled with the geometric estimates
||{\mathbf{n}}-{\mathbf{n}}_{h}||_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{h})}\leq ch^{k_{g}}
(
4.15
), the time-inequalities (
6.13
), and (
7.5
), the bound
||\bm{H}_{h}||_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{h})}\leq c
(see (
4.15
)), and, lastly, for the last difference in (
7.1
) we use
Lemma

4.15
with
\beta=(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell}\cdot{\mathbf{n}_{\Gamma}}
, and
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}=\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n})^{-\ell}\in\bm{H}^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})
.

Regarding
\mathrm{C}_{2}^{cov}
we readily see that

\displaystyle|\mathrm{C}_{2}^{cov}|=\frac{1}{2}\Big|\int_{\Gamma^{n}}\big(({\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n}))\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}^{cov}{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\big)\cdot(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell}\Big|+\frac{1}{2}\Big|\int_{\Gamma^{n}}\big(({\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n}))\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}^{cov}\big){\mathbf{P}_{\Gamma}}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\Big|.

Then, following exact calculations as for
\mathrm{C}_{2}
beforehand, where we have replaced
\nabla_{\Gamma}
with
\nabla_{\Gamma}^{cov}
, along with the fact that
||\nabla_{\Gamma}^{cov}{\mathbf{P}_{\Gamma}}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma)}\leq c||{\mathbf{P}_{\Gamma}}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell}||_{a}\leq c||\mathbf{v}_{h}||_{a_{h}}
by the perturbation bound (
4.77
) and the
H^{1}
coercivity bound (
4.54
), the following holds

(7.27)

\displaystyle|\mathrm{C}_{2}|\leq c\sqrt{\Delta t}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t_{n}}||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+h^{2(\widehat{r}_{u})}||{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\Big)^{1/2}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}}.

See
[
33
, Lemma 7.4]
where this exact calculations are performed in more detail for the stationary case. Combining then, (
7.1
), (
7.27
) into (
7.1
) gives the final result.
∎

Lemma 7.4

(Inertia errors)
.

The following bounds are satisfied, for all
\mathbf{v}_{h}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}

(7.28)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|

\displaystyle\leq c\big(||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}^{1/2}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}+||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\big)||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

(7.29)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{C}^{cov}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|

\displaystyle\leq c\big(||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}^{1/2}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}+||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}+||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{1/2}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}\big)||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}}.

In case where
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}=\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}
, considering the skew-symmetric nature of the inertia term, we have

(7.30)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{C}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})|\leq c(||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}^{1/2}||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}+||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}^{1/2}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}+||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})})||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

(7.31)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{C}^{cov}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})|\leq c(||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}^{1/2}||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}+||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}^{1/2}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}+||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}})||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}.

Proof.

Let us start with (
7.28
), then due to the skew-symmetric nature of the inertia term, as defined in
Definition

4.1
, (
7.30
) will be apparent. Recall that
\mathcal{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h})=-c_{h}(\underline{{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}}(\cdot,t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-c_{h}(\underline{\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}}(\cdot,t_{n});{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}),
then by the continuity bound (
4.65
) and time inequalities (
7.6
) and (
6.13
) we see that

\displaystyle c_{h}(\underline{{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}}(\cdot,t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})\leq||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}^{1/2}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

and similarly, using the stability estimates for the discrete velocity (
6.24
) as well, we obtain

\displaystyle c_{h}(\underline{\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}}(\cdot,t_{n});{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})\leq c||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

thus proving the initial bound (
7.28
).

Now choosing
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}=\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n,div}
, recalling the decomposition (
7.1
), the above estimate and the skew-symmetric form of
c_{h}(\bullet;\bullet,\bullet)
(see
Definition

4.1
), the final result (
7.30
) indeed holds true:

\displaystyle|\mathcal{C}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})|

\displaystyle=|c_{h}(\underline{{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}}(\cdot,t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-c_{h}(\underline{\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}}(\cdot,t_{n});\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n};\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})-\overbrace{c_{h}(\underline{\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}}(\cdot,t_{n});\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n};\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})}^{=0}|

\displaystyle\leq\big(||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}^{1/2}||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}+||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}^{1/2}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}\big)||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

\displaystyle+c||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}.

Regarding
\mathcal{C}^{cov}(\cdot)
, it is clear that if we use the bound (
4.64
) instead, the analogous results follow.
∎

Now we are ready to show error estimates involving the velocity for either the (
eNSWdh
) or the (
eNSWch
) scheme when
\underline{k_{\lambda}=k_{u}}
.

Lemma 7.5
.

Assume
\underline{k_{\lambda}=k_{u}}
, and that the regularity
1
hold.
Let
({\mathbf{u}},\{p,\lambda\})
be the solution of (
eNSWd
) or (
eNSWc
) and
(\mathbf{u}_{h}^{k},\{p_{h}^{k},\lambda_{h}^{k}\})
,
k=1,...,n
be the discrete solutions of (
eNSWdh
) or (
eNSWch
) respectively, with initial condition
\mathbf{u}_{h}^{0}=\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}
. Then, for sufficiently small time-step
\Delta t
, the following estimate holds for
1\leq n\leq N
:

(7.32)

\displaystyle||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}+\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{k}||_{a_{h}}^{2}\leq C_{const}\big(\Delta t\big)^{2}+C_{int}\big(h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}}+h^{2k_{pr}+2}+h^{2k_{\lambda}+2}\big),

(7.33)

\displaystyle\text{with }\qquad C_{cons}=c

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}||\partial^{\circ}\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt,

\displaystyle C_{int}=C_{stab}+c\int_{0}^{T}

\displaystyle||\partial^{\circ}{{\mathbf{u}}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt+c\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\big\{||{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||p||_{H^{k_{pr}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||\lambda||_{H^{k_{\lambda}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\big\},

and
\widehat{r}_{u}=min\{k_{u},k_{g}\}
, where
C_{stab}
the constant in (
6.24
).

Proof.

Testing the error equation (
EQ
) with
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}=\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n,div}
and recalling (
6.8
) (see also calculations in
Lemma

6.3
) we see that straightforward calculations and re-arrangements give

(7.34)

\displaystyle

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\Big({\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\underline{\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}}(t^{n-1}))\Big)}={\dfrac{1}{2\Delta t}\Big({\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})\Big)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{\Delta t}{2}{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\underline{\partial^{\circ}_{h,\tau}\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}}(t^{n-1}),\underline{\partial^{\circ}_{h,\tau}\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}}(t^{n-1}))}_{\geq 0}+\frac{1}{2\Delta t}\underbrace{\Big({\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\underline{\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}}(t^{n-1}),\underline{\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}}(t^{n-1}))\Big)}_{\leq\Delta t||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}\text{ by }\eqref{eq: time differences properties bilinear m ENS}},

and therefore we derive

(7.35)

\displaystyle{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})

\displaystyle+2\Delta t\,a_{S,h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq 2\Delta t||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\Delta t\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{5}\textsc{Err}_{i}^{C}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\textsc{Err}_{i}^{I}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})+\mathcal{C}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})\Big),

where, since
\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n,div}
, by the Ritz-Stokes projection defined in (
5.6
),
\textsc{Err}^{5}_{C}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})={\mathbf{m}}({\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})^{\ell})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})=G_{{\mathbf{m}}}({\mathbf{u}}^{n},\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})+{\mathbf{m}}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})\leq ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1/2}||{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}
, due to the bounds (
4.71
) and (
5.15
), (
5.16
). Considering, now, the consistency bounds in
Lemma

7.3
, the fact that
||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h})}\leq||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}
, cf. (
4.70
), and the Ritz-Stokes bound
||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\leq ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1/2}||{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}
, cf. (
5.15
), (
5.16
), using Young’s inequality appropriately we obtain

\displaystyle|\sum_{i=1}^{5}\textsc{Err}_{i}^{C}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})|

\displaystyle\leq c\Delta t\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)+ch^{2k_{g}}\big(||{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n-1})}^{2}+||p^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}^{2}

(7.36)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!+||\lambda^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{n})}^{2}+||{\bm{f}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{n})}^{2}\big)+\frac{ch^{2\widehat{r}_{u}+2}}{\Delta t}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!+ch^{2\widehat{r}_{u}+2}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sup_{t\in[t_{n-1},t_{n}]}||{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+ch^{2\widehat{r}_{u}+1}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}^{2}+c||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}+\frac{1}{5}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}^{2}.

Similarly, using instead the interpolation errors in (
7.9
) and (
7.1
) we find

\displaystyle|\sum_{i=1}^{2}\textsc{Err}_{i}^{I}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})|

\displaystyle\leq\big(\frac{h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}}+h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}+2}}{\Delta t}+\Delta th^{2\widehat{r}_{u}}\big)\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}\sum_{j=0}^{1}||(\partial^{\circ})^{j}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt

(7.37)

\displaystyle+c(h^{2k_{pr}+2}+h^{2k_{\lambda}+2})\big(||p^{n}||_{H^{k_{pr}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}^{2}+||\lambda^{n}||_{H^{k_{\lambda}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}^{2}\big)+\frac{1}{5}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}.

Lastly, considering (
7.30
), again with the help of the Ritz-Stokes bounds (
5.13
), (
5.15
) and simple application of Young’s inequality we find

(7.38)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{C}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})|\leq c(1+||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}})h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}}+c||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}^{2}+\frac{1}{5}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{2}+\frac{1}{5}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}^{2}.

Now, plugging (
7.1
)-(
7.38
) into (
7.35
), considering
\Delta t
sufficiently small, applying kickback arguments where appropriate, and taking into account appropriate inclusions, e.g.
\int_{0}^{T}||{{\mathbf{u}}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\leq c(T)\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\{||{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma(t))}\}
, we obtain upon summing for
k=1,...,n
that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}+\frac{1}{5}\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{k}||_{a_{h}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq c\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{k}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{k})}^{2}+C_{cons}(\Delta t)^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{int}(h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}}+h^{2k_{pr}+2}+h^{2k_{\lambda}+2})+\big(\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{k-1}||_{a_{h}}\big)\,h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}},

with
C_{cons}
and
C_{int}
as in (
7.33
) and where we also used the fact that
\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{0}=0
, by choice of initial condition
\mathbf{u}_{h}^{0}=\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}
. Applying a discrete Grönwall argument (assuming
\Delta t
small enough), using the stability estimate
\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}\leq C_{stab}
, where
C_{stab}
the constant in (
6.24
), and simple simplifications concerning the previously mentioned constants, yield our desired estimate.
∎

Lemma 7.6
.

Assume
\underline{k_{\lambda}=k_{u}-1}
,
k_{g}\geq 2
and that the regularity
1
hold.
Let
({\mathbf{u}},\{p,\lambda\})
be the solution of (
eNSWc
) and
(\mathbf{u}_{h}^{k},\{p_{h}^{k},\lambda_{h}^{k}\})
,
k=1,...,n
be the discrete solutions of (
eNSWch
), with initial condition
\mathbf{u}_{h}^{0}=\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}
. Then, for sufficiently small time-step
\Delta t
, the following estimate holds for
1\leq n\leq N
:

(7.39)

\displaystyle||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}+\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{k}||_{a_{h}}^{2}\leq C_{const}\big(\Delta t\big)^{2}+C_{int}\big(h^{2r_{u}}+h^{2k_{pr}+2}+h^{2k_{\lambda}+2}\big).

where
r_{u}=min\{k_{u},k_{g}-1\}
, with constants
C_{conc}
,
C_{int}
as in
Lemma

7.5
.

Proof.

The proof follows almost identically as in
Lemma

7.5
, where one now makes use of the bounds (
7.7
) for
\textsc{Err}_{1}^{I}
and (
7.17
) for
\textsc{Err}_{3}^{C,cov}
instead of (
7.9
) and (
7.16
) respectively, the worse Ritz-Stokes estimates (
5.11
),(
5.40
) and lastly the inverse inequality
||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\leq h^{-1}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}
instead of the improved
H^{1}
coercivity bound (
4.13
), where appropriate.
∎

Remark 7.2

(About pressure stability)
.

Using the modified Ritz-Stokes projection (
5.6
), we see with the help of the inverse inequality that

(7.40)

||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}}\leq||\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}+||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}\leq||\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}+h^{-1}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}.

Bearing in mind the
1
, the previously proven estimate (
7.32
), and the stability of the Ritz-Stokes map (
5.8
), we see that the following holds true:

(7.41)

\displaystyle\sup_{n}||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}}\leq C_{a},\qquad\text{ if }\Delta t\leq ch.

This is exactly the condition (
6.30
), which we needed to prove the
L^{2}_{L^{2}}\times L^{2}_{H_{h}^{-1}}
pressure stability in
Lemma

6.8
. Notice, that the time-step restriction is reasonable since it will be automatically satisfied if we try to balance spatial and temporal discretization errors, as this leads to
\Delta t\sim h^{k_{u}}
.

Remark 7.3

(About
L^{2}_{L^{2}}\times L^{2}_{L^{2}}
pressure stability for
k_{\lambda}-1
)
.

As mentioned before in
Remark

6.6
, to prove
L^{2}_{L^{2}}\times L^{2}_{L^{2}}
pressure stability for
k_{\lambda}-1
, we need an
L^{\infty}
-uniform bound instead, cf. (
6.47
). For this to hold, however, we need further regularity assumption for the velocity, namely
{\mathbf{u}}\in L^{\infty}_{W^{2,\infty}(\Gamma(t))}
. To see this, by similar calculations as in (
7.40
), where instead we use the
L^{\infty}
-bound of the Ritz-Stokes projection (
5.19
), we find that (assuming that
\Delta t\leq ch
and
k_{g}\geq 2
)

(7.42)

\displaystyle||\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\leq||\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma^{n})}\leq||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{W^{2,\infty}(\Gamma^{n})}+h^{-1}||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

and therefore
\sup_{n}||{\mathbf{u}}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\leq||{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{\infty}_{W^{2,\infty}(\Gamma(t))}}\!\!\!\!+C_{int}+C_{cons}\leq C_{a}^{\infty}
, i.e. (
6.47
).

Now, factoring in the above discrete remainder bounds (
7.32
) and (
7.39
), the decomposition (
7.1
), the triangle’s inequality and the Ritz-Stokes estimates (
5.13
), the following
main result
readily holds.

Theorem 7.7

(Velocity error bounds)
.

Under the Assumption
1
and
Lemma

7.5
the following velocity error estimates hold for
1\leq n\leq N

(7.43)

\displaystyle||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}+\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{k}||_{a_{h}}^{2}\leq C_{const}\big(\Delta t\big)^{2}+C_{int}\big(h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}}+h^{2k_{pr}+2}+h^{2k_{\lambda}+2}\big),

while under the assumptions of
Lemma

7.6
, instead, we have the following for
1\leq n\leq N

(7.44)

\displaystyle||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}+\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{k}||_{a_{h}}^{2}\leq C_{const}\big(\Delta t\big)^{2}+C_{int}\big(h^{2r_{u}}+h^{2k_{pr}+2}+h^{2k_{\lambda}+2}\big),

where
\widehat{r}_{u}=min\{k_{u},k_{g}\}
,
r_{u}=min\{k_{u},k_{g}-1\}
, with
C_{const},\,C_{int}
the constants in
Lemma

7.5
.

7.2.
Pressure a-priori error bounds for
\underline{k_{\lambda}=k_{u}}

We now analyze pressure results for
\underline{k_{\lambda}=k_{u}}
and the fully discrete scheme (
eNSWdh
) (similar results would hold for the alternative scheme (
eNSWch
)).
That means in our error equation (
EQ
) we use the estimates for
\textsc{Err}_{3}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})
and
\mathcal{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})
.

To establish a-priori estimates for the two pressures, we shall make use of the ideas introduced in the stability analysis
Section

6.4
. That is, utilize the
discrete Leray time-projection
(
6.17
) in combination with the
discrete inverse Stokes operator
to
\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}:{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n,div}\rightarrow{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n,div}
(
6.31
), to first establish an auxiliary bound on the
time-projected
fully discrete time derivative in a negative energy norm, and then by the duality estimate in
Lemma

6.5
and the discrete
L^{2}\times H_{h}^{-1}

inf-sup
condition (
6.41
) provide optimal error bounds for the two pressures (albeit in a weaker norm for
\lambda_{h}
).

Lemma 7.8

(Auxiliary result)
.

Assume
\underline{k_{\lambda}=k_{u}}
, and let the regularity
1
hold with time-step
\Delta t\leq ch
. Let
({\mathbf{u}},\{p,\lambda\})
be the solution of (
eNSWd
) and
(\mathbf{u}_{h}^{k},\{p_{h}^{k},\lambda_{h}^{k}\})
,
k=1,...,n
be the discrete solutions of (
eNSWdh
), with initial condition
\mathbf{u}_{h}^{0}=\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}
. Then, recalling the bound (
7.41
), the following estimate holds for
1\leq n\leq N

(7.45)

\sum_{k=1}^{n}\Delta t||\frac{\mathcal{A}_{h}^{k}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{k}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{k-1})}{\Delta t}||_{a_{h}}^{2}\leq C_{const}\big(\Delta t\big)^{2}+C_{int}\big(h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}}+h^{2k_{pr}+2}+h^{2k_{\lambda}+2}\big),

where
\widehat{r}_{u}=min\{k_{u},k_{g}\}
, with
C_{const},\,C_{int}
the constants in
Lemma

7.5
.

Proof.

First, recall the
discrete Leray time-projection
defined in (
6.17
), then by (
6.31
) it is clear that
\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n,div}
is well-posed (since
\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n,div}
for all
n\geq 0
by (
6.17
)). So, testing (
EQ
) with
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}=\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n,div}
, and applying (
6.17
), yields for
1\leq n\leq N

\displaystyle\!\!\!\big(\frac{\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}{\Delta t},\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})\big)_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+a_{h}(\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n},\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}))\leq 2\Big(|\sum_{i=1}^{5}\textsc{Err}_{i}^{C}(\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}))|

(7.46)

\displaystyle\!\!\!+|\sum_{i=1}^{2}\textsc{Err}_{i}^{I}(\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}))|+|\mathcal{C}(\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}))|\Big),

where we again we use that by the Ritz-Stokes projection defined in (
5.6
)
|\textsc{Err}_{5}^{C}(\mathcal{A}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}))|\leq h^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1/2}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathcal{A}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}
(see below (
7.35
) in
Lemma

7.5
). Now, we bound each term appropriately as in
Lemma

6.4
with the help
of Young’s inequality
in the following manner:

\bullet\
By the definition of
\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\cdot)
(
6.31
) we obtain

\displaystyle\ \big(\frac{\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}{\Delta t},\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})\big)_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}=\frac{1}{\Delta t}||\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})||^{2}_{a_{h}}.

\bullet\
The second term in (
7.2
) can be bounded as

\displaystyle a_{S,h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}))\leq c\Delta t||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}^{2}+\frac{1}{5\Delta t}||\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})||_{a_{h}}^{2}.

\bullet\
Using
Lemma

7.2
(similar to (
7.1
)) we obtain the following bound for the interpolation error

\displaystyle|\sum_{i=1}^{2}\textsc{Err}_{i}^{I}(\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}))|\leq c\Delta t\big(\frac{h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}}+h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}+2}}{\Delta t}+\Delta th^{2\widehat{r}_{u}}\big)\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}{{\mathbf{u}}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt

\displaystyle+c\Delta t(h^{2k_{pr}+2}+h^{2k_{\lambda}+2})\big(||{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}^{2}+||p||_{H^{k_{pr}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}^{2}+||\lambda||_{H^{k_{\lambda}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}^{2}\big)+\frac{1}{5\Delta t}||\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})||^{2}_{a_{h}}.

\bullet\
For the consistency error by
Lemma

7.3
(similar to (
7.1
)) and the
H^{1}
-coercivity (
4.70
) we obtain

\displaystyle|\sum_{i=1}^{5}\textsc{Err}_{i}^{C}(\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}))|\leq c(\Delta t)^{2}\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt+c\Delta th^{2\widehat{r}_{u}+1}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+c\Delta t||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}+c\Delta th^{2k_{g}}\big(||{\bm{f}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{n})}^{2}+||p^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}^{2}+||\lambda^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{n})}^{2}\big)+\frac{1}{5\Delta t}||\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})||^{2}_{a_{h}}.

\bullet\
Finally, recalling the uniform bound (
7.40
), we can calculate the inertia error term using (
7.28
)     in
Lemma

7.4
, and the
H^{1}
-coercivity (
4.70
), to find

\displaystyle|\mathcal{C}(\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}))|\leq\Delta t||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}+C_{a}\Delta t||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}+\frac{1}{5\Delta t}||\mathcal{A}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})||_{a_{h}}^{2},

where
C_{a}
the constant in (
7.41
). Notice, also that by the Ritz-Stokes estimate (
5.15
) and (
4.70
)

\phantom{aaaa}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\leq||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\leq

ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}+||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}
.

So, combining the above into (
7.2
) and applying a kickback argument against
\frac{1}{\Delta t}||\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})||^{2}_{a_{h}}
upon summing over
k=1,...,n
and using the velocity estimates (
7.32
), (
7.44
), completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 7.9
.

Assume
\underline{k_{\lambda}=k_{u}}
, and let the regularity
1
hold with time-step
\Delta t\leq ch
. Let
({\mathbf{u}},\{p,\lambda\})
be the solution of (
eNSWd
) and
(\mathbf{u}_{h}^{k},\{p_{h}^{k},\lambda_{h}^{k}\})
,
k=1,...,n
be the discrete solutions of (
eNSWdh
), with initial condition
\mathbf{u}_{h}^{0}=\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}
. Then, recalling (
7.41
), the following holds for
1\leq n\leq N

(7.47)

\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||\{\theta_{p}^{k},\theta_{\lambda}^{k}\}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{k})\times H_{h}^{-1}(\Gamma_{h}^{k})}^{2}\leq C_{const}\big(\Delta t\big)^{2}+C_{int}\big(h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}}+h^{2k_{pr}+2}+h^{2k_{\lambda}+2}\big),

where
\widehat{r}_{u}=min\{k_{u},k_{g}\}
, with
C_{const},\,C_{int}
the constants in
Lemma

7.5
.

Proof.

First, let us recall the discrete
L^{2}\times H_{h}^{-1}

inf-sup
condition (
6.41
) to see that

(7.48)

\displaystyle||\{\theta_{p}^{n},\theta_{\lambda}^{n}\}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})\times H_{h}^{-1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\leq\sup_{\mathbf{v}_{h}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n}}\frac{b_{h}^{L}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n},\{\theta_{p}^{n},\theta_{\lambda}^{n}\})}{||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h})}}.

Solving (
EQ
) for
b_{h}^{L}(\mathbf{v}_{h},\{\theta_{p}^{n},\theta_{\lambda}^{n}\})
with
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n}
, and using the Ritz-Stokes projection defined in (
5.6
) to obtain
|\textsc{Err}_{5}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|\leq|b_{h}^{L}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n},\mathcal{P}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}),\mathcal{L}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}))|+ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1/2}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}
(see calculations below (
7.35
) in
Lemma

7.5
again), we find that

\displaystyle b_{h}^{L}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n},\{\theta_{p}^{n},\theta_{\lambda}^{n}\})\leq\Big|{\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t_{n-1}))}\big|+a_{S,h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})+|b_{h}^{L}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n},\mathcal{P}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}),\mathcal{L}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}))|

(7.49)

\displaystyle+ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1/2}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{4}|\textsc{Err}_{i}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|+\sum_{i=1}^{2}|\textsc{Err}_{i}^{I}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|+|\mathcal{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|\Big).

We focus on the first and third term of the above eq. (
7.2
), since the rest of the terms have already been handled before (see
Lemmas

7.2
,
7.3
and
7.4
and
Theorem

7.7
). As in the stability results
Lemma

6.8
using the dual estimate (
6.5
) we arrive at

(7.50)

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n}}\dfrac{\frac{1}{\Delta t}\Big({\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t_{n-1}))\Big)}{||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}}\leq c||\frac{\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})}{\Delta t}||_{a_{h}}+c||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}.

On the other hand, using the Ritz-Stokes bound (
5.13
), the super-approximation property (
5.4
) and the interpolation estimates (
5.1
) for
\widetilde{I}_{h}^{SZ}
and the
H_{h}^{-1}
-norm definition (
4.6
), yields

(7.51)

\displaystyle

\displaystyle|b_{h}^{L}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n},\mathcal{P}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}),\mathcal{L}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}))|\leq||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}}||\mathcal{P}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{n})||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+\big|m_{h}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\cdot({\mathbf{n}}_{h}-{\mathbf{n}}),\mathcal{L}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{n}))\big|

\displaystyle+\big|m_{h}(\widetilde{I}_{h}^{SZ}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\cdot{\mathbf{n}}),\mathcal{L}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{n}))\big|+\big|m_{h}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\cdot{\mathbf{n}}-\widetilde{I}_{h}^{SZ}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\cdot{\mathbf{n}}),\mathcal{L}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{n}))\big|

\displaystyle\leq||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}}||\mathcal{P}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{n})||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+ch||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}||\mathcal{L}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{n})||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}||\mathcal{L}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{n})||_{H_{h}^{-1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

\displaystyle\leq ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}.

So, plugging first (
7.2
) into the
inf-sup
condition (
7.48
), using (
7.50
), (
7.51
) and the already proven bounds in
Lemma

7.8
,
Lemma

7.2
,
Lemma

7.3
,
Lemma

7.4

\big(
specifically for (
7.28
), by the Ritz-Stokes estimate (
5.15
) we have
||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\leq||\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\leq

h^{\widehat{r}_{u}}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}+||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}\!\big)
, applying the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and factor out
||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}
(since
||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\leq||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}}\leq||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}
), after some tedious (but already carried out earlier) calculations we derive

\displaystyle||\{\theta_{p}^{n},\theta_{\lambda}^{n}\}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})\times H_{h}^{-1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\leq||\frac{\mathcal{A}_{h}^{n}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\hat{\theta}_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})}{\Delta t}||_{a_{h}}+c||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}+c||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}+ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1/2}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}

\displaystyle+c\sqrt{\Delta t}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}+h^{k_{g}}\big(||{\bm{f}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{n})}+||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}+||p^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}

\displaystyle+||\lambda^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{n})}\big)+||\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+\dfrac{c}{\sqrt{\Delta t}}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}\sum_{j=0}^{1}||(\partial^{\circ}_{h})^{j}\rho_{{\mathbf{u}}}(t)||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}+c\big(h^{k_{pr}+1}

\displaystyle+h^{k_{\lambda}+1}\big)\big(||p^{n}||_{H^{k_{pr}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}+||\lambda^{n}||_{H^{k_{\lambda}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}\big)+c||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}+c||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}+C_{a}h^{\widehat{r}_{u}}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma^{n})}.

Then, squaring and applying the operator
\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}
and using the Ritz-Stokes estimates (
5.16
), (
5.15
), (
5.42
), along with the auxiliary result (
7.45
) in
Lemma

7.8
and the velocity estimates in
Lemma

7.5
and
Theorem

7.7
we obtain our desired result.
∎

Now, by the decompositions (
7.2
), (
7.3
), the interpolation bounds (
7.4
), and a simple application of the triangle inequality, one may then derive the following
main result
regarding the pressure errors.

Theorem 7.10

(Pressure error bounds)
.

Under the regularity assumption
1
and
Lemma

7.9
the following pressure error estimates holds for
1\leq n\leq N

(7.52)

\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||\{{\mathbf{e}}_{p}^{k},{\mathbf{e}}_{\lambda}^{k}\}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{k})\times H_{h}^{-1}(\Gamma_{h}^{k})}^{2}\leq C_{const}\big(\Delta t\big)^{2}+C_{int}\big(h^{2\widehat{r}_{u}}+h^{2k_{pr}+2}+h^{2k_{\lambda}+2}\big),

where
\widehat{r}_{u}=min\{k_{u},k_{g}\}
, with
C_{const},\,C_{int}
the constants in
Lemma

7.5
.

7.3.
Pressure a-priori error bounds for
\underline{k_{\lambda}=k_{u}-1}

Now we seek to prove a-priori pressure estimates when
k_{\lambda}=k_{u}-1
. As mentioned in
Section

6.2
and more specifically in
Remark

6.2
we now consider the continuous weak formulation (
eNSWc
) and its fully discrete finite element approximation (
eNSWch
).

There is one main difference between this case and
Section

7.2
, namely the coercivity estimate in
Lemma

4.13
and the dual estimate in
Lemma

6.5
do not hold for
k_{\lambda}=k_{u}-1
; see
[
33
]
for the stationary case. As a result, it is not possible to follow the steps in
Section

7.2
.
So, as mentioned in
Remark

6.6
, we need to find an error bound for the
L^{2}_{L^{2}}
-norm of the fully discrete time derivative, that is, the expression

(7.53)

\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\partial^{\circ}_{h,\tau}\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\partial^{\circ}_{h,\tau}\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})=\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}),\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}))}{(\Delta t)^{2}},

where we recall
\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}=\underline{\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(\cdot,t_{n})+\Delta t\,\partial^{\circ}_{h,\tau}\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}
by (
6.8
). To achieve this, one could “just” test the error eq. (
EQ
) with
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}=\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n})\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n}
, since we can rewrite the time-derivative approximation as

(7.54)

{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t^{n-1}))={\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}),\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})+\big[{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\underline{\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}),\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\theta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t^{n-1}))\big],

but it would not be possible to find optimal convergence rates. Indeed, the test functions
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}
in the error bounds
(
7.8
), (
7.17
), (
7.29
) are with respect to the energy
(a_{h})
-norm (
4.38
), which cannot be controlled (e.g. using Young’s inequality and a kickback argument) by (
7.53
), unless an inverse inequality is applied, hence losing an order of convergence. To alleviate this issue, we must utilize test functions w.r.t. the
L^{2}
-norm.
This is accomplished through a combination of two factors:

i) We use the standard Ritz-Stokes projection
(\mathcal{R}_{h}^{b}({\mathbf{u}}^{n}),\{\mathcal{P}_{h}^{b}({\mathbf{u}}^{n}),\mathcal{L}_{h}^{b}({\mathbf{u}}^{n})\})
defined in (
5.7
) instead. With this adjustment, we manage to remove one of the aforementioned problematic bounds (
7.8
) involving the bilinear form
b_{h}^{L}
. To make things simpler, we will also assume
new initial conditions
for the discrete scheme (
eNSWch
), mainly that
\mathbf{u}_{h}^{0}=\mathcal{R}_{h}^{b}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}=\mathcal{R}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{0},\{p^{0},\lambda^{0}\})
. This is done so that at time-step
t_{0}=0
the
discrete remainder
defined below in (
7.55
) remains zero,

\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{0}=0
, and so the calculations follow identical to
Section

7.1
for
k_{\lambda}=k_{u}-1
, see also
Remark

7.4
below.

ii) We use the integration by part formula (
4.56
) for the inertia terms (
7.17
), (
7.29
), so that test functions are bounded w.r.t. the
L^{2}
-norm instead.

All of the above changes come at the expense of slightly higher regularity assumptions, compared to
Section

7.2
, which are the following:

Assumptions 2

(Regularity II)
.

We assume that the solution
({\mathbf{u}},\{p,\lambda\})
has the following regularity

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\big\{\sum_{j=0}^{1}||(\partial^{\circ})^{j}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}

\displaystyle+||(\partial^{\circ})^{j}p||_{H^{k_{pr}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||(\partial^{\circ})^{j}\lambda||_{H^{k_{\lambda}+1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\big\}

\displaystyle+\sup_{t\in[0,T]}||{\mathbf{u}}||_{W^{2,\infty}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}||\partial^{\circ}\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\leq C.

Considering the standard Ritz-Stokes projection (
5.4
) we use the following new decompositions:

(7.55)

\displaystyle{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}={\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{({\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\mathcal{R}_{h}^{b}({\mathbf{u}}^{n}))}_{\text{Interpolation error}}+\underbrace{(\mathcal{R}_{h}^{b}({\mathbf{u}}^{n})-\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n})}_{\text{discrete remainder}}:=\eta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}+\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},

\displaystyle\{{\mathbf{e}}_{p}^{n},{\mathbf{e}}_{\lambda}^{n}\}=\{p^{n},\lambda^{n}\}-\{p_{h}^{n},\lambda_{h}^{n}\}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{(\{p^{n},\lambda^{n}\}-\{\mathcal{P}_{h}^{b}({\mathbf{u}}^{n}),\mathcal{L}_{h}^{b}({\mathbf{u}}^{n})\}}_{\text{Interpolation error}}+\underbrace{(\{\mathcal{P}_{h}^{b}({\mathbf{u}}^{n}),\mathcal{L}_{h}^{b}({\mathbf{u}}^{n})\}-\{p_{h},\lambda_{h}\})}_{\text{discrete remainder}}

(7.56)

\displaystyle:=\eta_{\{p,\lambda\}}^{n}+\sigma_{\{p,\lambda\}}^{n}.

The bounds on the Ritz-Stokes interpolation error have already been proved in
Lemma

5.4
,
Lemma

5.9
. Following, now, the calculation as in
Lemma

7.1
, due to the new pressure decompositions (
7.3
), the new initial condition
\mathbf{u}_{h}^{0}=\mathcal{R}_{h}^{b}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}
, and utilizing the fact that
a_{h}=a_{S,h}+{\mathbf{m}}_{h}
, cf.
Definition

4.1
, we derive the new error equation, which is presented below.

Lemma 7.11
.

For
\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\,\sigma_{\{p,\lambda\}}^{n}
as in (
7.55
), (
7.3
), the following error equations holds true for
n\geq 1
:

(EQ
c
)

\displaystyle\boxed{\begin{cases}&{\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\Big({\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t^{n-1}))\Big)}+a_{h}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})+b_{h}^{L}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n},\sigma_{\{p,\lambda\}}^{n})\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad~~=\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{3}\textsc{Err}_{i}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})}_{\textsc{Cons. Errors}}+\underbrace{\textsc{Err}_{1}^{I}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})}_{\textsc{Inter. Errors}}+\!\!\!\!\!\underbrace{\mathcal{C}^{cov}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})}_{\textsc{Inert. Errors}}\!\!\!+\,{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}),\\
&b_{h}^{L}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\{q_{h}^{n},\xi_{h}^{n}\})=0,\end{cases}}

for all
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n}
and
\{q_{h}^{n},\xi_{h}^{n}\}\in Q_{h}^{n}\times\Lambda_{h}^{n}
,
with
consistency errors
:

•

\textsc{Err}_{1}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}):=\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\Big({\mathbf{m}}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}({\mathbf{u}}^{n-1},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t_{n-1}))\Big)-{\mathbf{m}}(\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})-{\mathbf{g}}_{h}({\mathbf{V}}_{\Gamma_{h}}^{N};\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})

•

\textsc{Err}_{2}^{C,cov}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}):=c_{h}^{cov}(\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-c^{cov}({\mathbf{u}}^{n};{\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})

•

\textsc{Err}_{3}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}):={\mathbf{m}}({\bm{f}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}({\bm{f}}_{h}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})

and
interpolations errors
:

•

\textsc{Err}_{1}^{I}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}):=\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\Big({\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\eta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\eta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(t_{n-1}))\Big)

•

\mathcal{C}^{cov}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}):=c_{h}^{cov}(\underline{{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-c_{h}^{cov}(\underline{\mathbf{u}_{h}^{n-1}}(t_{n});{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n};\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})

•

Now, due to the choice of initial condition, we see from
Section

7.1
,by following almost identical calculations to
Lemma

7.6
and
Theorem

7.7
that the following velocity error estimates hold:

(7.57)

\displaystyle||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}+\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{k}||_{a_{h}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(\big(\Delta t\big)^{2}+h^{2r_{u}}+h^{2k_{pr}+2}+h^{2k_{\lambda}+2}\Big),

(7.58)

\displaystyle||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}+\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{k}||_{a_{h}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(\big(\Delta t\big)^{2}+h^{2r_{u}}+h^{2k_{pr}+2}+h^{2k_{\lambda}+2}\Big),

where
r_{u}=min\{k_{u},k_{g}-1\}
, with positive constant
C
depending on the regularity
2
, and where for the last estimate we used the standard Ritz-Stokes interpolation estimates (
5.4
), (
5.62
).

Remark 7.4
.

Let us discuss, once more, the initial condition and the condition (
7.42
):

i)

As mentioned, we choose
\mathbf{u}_{h}^{0}=\mathcal{R}_{h}^{b}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}
so that
\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{0}=0
. Therefore
the computations follow almost identical to
Section

7.1
, giving the estimates (
7.57
), (
7.58
). However, in practice, see
Remark

7.1
, this is not convenient and therefore a more standard choice would have been
\mathbf{u}_{h}^{0}=\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}
. In that case, the term
||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{0}||_{a_{h}}=||\mathcal{R}_{h}^{b}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}-\mathcal{R}_{h}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}||_{a_{h}}\neq 0
would emerge at
t=0
, but due to the linearity of the Ritz-Stokes projection (
5.7
) we see that
||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{0}||_{a_{h}}=||\mathcal{R}_{h}(0,\{p^{0},\lambda^{0}\})||_{a_{h}}\leq c(h^{k_{pr}+1}+h^{k_{\lambda}+1}+h^{k_{g}})(||p||_{H^{k_{pr}+1}}+||\lambda||_{H^{k_{\lambda}+1}})
and hence the error bounds would still hold.

ii)

By the
L^{\infty}
stability of the standard Ritz-Stokes map (
5.19
) and (
7.57
),

Remark

7.3
still stands.

Now, let us revisit the consistency, interpolation and inertia bounds. Following the similar calculation to
Lemma

7.2
, where we use the Ritz-Stokes estimates (
5.9
) we obtain the following.

Lemma 7.12

(Interpolation errors II)
.

The following interpolation error hold for all
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n}

(7.59)

\displaystyle|\textsc{Err}^{I}_{1}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|

\displaystyle\leq ch^{r_{u}}\!\!\!\!\!\sup_{t\in[t_{n-1},t_{n}]}\sum_{j=0}^{1}\big(||(\partial^{\circ})^{j}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{k_{u}+1}(\Gamma(t))}\big)||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

\displaystyle+c(h^{k_{pr}+1}+h^{,k_{\lambda}+1})\!\!\!\!\!\sup_{t\in[t_{n-1},t_{n}]}\sum_{j=0}^{1}\big(||(\partial^{\circ})^{j}p||_{H^{k_{pr}+1}(\Gamma(t))}+||(\partial^{\circ})^{j}\lambda||_{H^{k_{\lambda}+1}(\Gamma(t))}\big)||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

with
r_{u}=min\{k_{u},k_{g}-1\}
and the constants
c>0
are independent of
h
and
t
.

Lemma 7.13

(Consistency errors II)
.

There exists a constant
c>0
independent of
h
and
t
such that the following bounds hold for all
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n}

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!|\textsc{Err}_{1}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|\leq c\sqrt{\Delta t}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}||\partial^{\circ}\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

(7.60)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\,\!\!\!\!+\frac{ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1}}{\sqrt{\Delta t}}\Big(\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t^{n}}+||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}+||{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}^{2}\,dt\Big)^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\,\!\!\!\!+ch^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sup_{t\in[t_{n-1},t_{n}]}\big(||{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}\big)||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+\big(||\eta_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\big)||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!|\textsc{Err}_{2}^{C,cov}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|\leq c(\Delta t+h^{\widehat{r}_{u}})\sup_{t\in I_{n}}\Big\{\sum_{j=1}^{0}||(\partial^{\circ})^{j}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}\Big\}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{W^{1,\infty}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

(7.61)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+ch^{k_{g}-1}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n-1})}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

(7.62)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!|\textsc{Err}_{3}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})|\leq ch^{k_{g}+1}||{\bm{f}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}.

Proof.

\textsc{Err}_{1}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})
and
\textsc{Err}_{3}^{C}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})
are obtained identically to
Lemma

7.3
. Let us focus on
\textsc{Err}^{C,cov}_{2}
. As mentioned, now we need to apply the integration by parts formula so that the test function is bounded w.r.t the
L^{2}
-norm. As in
Lemma

7.3
we write (
7.1
) again:

(7.63)

\displaystyle\textsc{Err}^{C,cov}_{2}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})

\displaystyle=c_{h}^{cov}(\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-c^{cov}(\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})

\displaystyle\qquad+c^{cov}(\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n});{\mathbf{u}}^{n},(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})-c^{cov}({\mathbf{u}}^{n};{\mathbf{u}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})

(7.64)

\displaystyle=:\mathrm{C}_{1}^{cov}+\mathrm{C}_{2}^{cov},

where we recall
c_{h}^{cov}
,
c^{cov}
as defined in
Definition

4.1
and (
3.13
) (with
\eta_{1},\eta_{2}=0
) respectively. Following (
7.22
), and also using the inverse inequality, yields

(7.65)

\displaystyle|\mathrm{C}_{1}^{cov}|\leq ch^{k_{g}-1}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n-1})}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}.

For the second estimate in (
7.63
) we proceed similarly to (
7.24
):

\displaystyle|\mathrm{C}_{2}^{cov}|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\Big|\int_{\Gamma^{n}}\big(({\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n}))\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}^{cov}{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\big)\cdot(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell}\Big|+\frac{1}{2}\Big|\int_{\Gamma^{n}}\big(({\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n}))\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma}^{cov}\big){\mathbf{P}_{\Gamma}}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})^{\ell})\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\Big|

(7.66)

\displaystyle=\textbf{I}c+\textbf{II}c,

where now using the further assumed regularity
2
and (
4.36
) we derive

\displaystyle\textbf{I}c\leq(\Delta t+h^{\widehat{r}_{u}+1})\sup_{t\in I_{n}}\big\{||\partial^{\circ}{\mathbf{u}}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma(t))}+||{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}\big\}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{W^{1,\infty}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}.

Recall that
{\mathbf{u}}
is the solution of (
eNSWc
), then by the integration by parts formula (
2.11
) we see that

(7.67)

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma^{n}}(({\mathbf{u}}^{n}\cdot\nabla^{cov}_{\Gamma^{n}}){\mathbf{P}_{\Gamma}}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell})\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\;d\sigma

\displaystyle=\int_{\Gamma^{n}}{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\cdot(\nabla_{\Gamma^{n}}(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{n}))\;d\sigma-\int_{\Gamma^{n}}(({\mathbf{u}}^{n}\cdot\nabla_{\Gamma^{n}}){\mathbf{u}}^{n})\cdot{\mathbf{P}_{\Gamma}}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell}\;d\sigma

\displaystyle=-\int_{\Gamma^{n}}\mathrm{div}_{\Gamma^{n}}({\mathbf{u}}^{n})(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{n})\;d\sigma-\int_{\Gamma^{n}}(({\mathbf{u}}^{n}\cdot\nabla^{cov}_{\Gamma^{n}}){\mathbf{u}}^{n})\cdot\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell}\;d\sigma,

where by
\nabla^{cov}_{\Gamma^{n}},\,\mathrm{div}_{\Gamma^{n}}
we denote the surface operators defined at the surface
\Gamma^{n}
.
So considering (
7.67
) it is clear that

\displaystyle\textbf{II}c=\textbf{I}c+\overbrace{\int_{\Gamma^{n}}\mathrm{div}_{\Gamma^{n}}({\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n})))(\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\ell}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{n})\;d\sigma}^{\textbf{III}c}.

Using then again (
7.23
), i.e.

{\mathbf{u}}^{n}-\underline{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}(t_{n})=\int_{t_{n-1}}^{t_{n}}\partial^{\circ}_{\ell}{\mathbf{u}}\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{s}\,ds\circ\Phi^{\ell}_{-t_{n}}

along with the pull back formula
[
17
, Section 3]

\mathrm{div}_{\Gamma^{n}}({\mathbf{u}}^{n})\circ\Phi_{t^{n}}^{\ell}=tr(\mathcal{U}_{t}^{0}\nabla_{\Gamma^{0}}{\mathbf{u}}^{n}\circ\Phi_{t^{n}}^{\ell})
, where
\mathcal{U}_{t}^{0}=\nabla_{\Gamma^{0}}\Phi_{t^{n}}^{\ell}\mathrm{G}_{0}^{t}
with
\mathrm{G}_{0}^{t}=(\mathrm{G}_{t}^{0})^{-1}=((\nabla_{\Gamma^{0}}\Phi_{t^{n}}^{\ell})^{t}\nabla_{\Gamma^{0}}\Phi_{t^{n}}^{\ell}+{\mathbf{n}}_{\Gamma^{0}}\otimes{\mathbf{n}}_{\Gamma^{0}})^{-1}=\phi_{-t}^{\ell}(\nabla_{\Gamma^{n}}\Phi_{-t^{n}}^{\ell})(\nabla_{\Gamma^{n}}\Phi_{-t^{n}}^{\ell})^{t}+{\mathbf{n}}_{\Gamma^{0}}\otimes{\mathbf{n}}_{\Gamma^{0}}
and the bounds on the mapping
\Phi_{t^{n}}^{\ell}
, we readily see that

\displaystyle\textbf{III}c\leq(\Delta t+h^{\widehat{r}_{u}})\sup_{t\in I_{n}}\Big\{\sum_{j=1}^{0}||(\partial^{\circ})^{j}{\mathbf{u}}||_{H^{1}(\Gamma(t))}\Big\}||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma^{n})}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})},

where we also applied (
4.36
). Combining now
\textbf{I}c-\textbf{III}c
into (
7.66
) coupled with (
7.65
), yields (
7.61
).
∎

Lemma 7.14

(Inertia error II)
.

The following error bound is satisfied

(7.68)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{C}^{cov}(\mathbf{v}_{h})|

\displaystyle\leq c||{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{W^{2,\infty}(\Gamma^{n})}\big(||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}+||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}+||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}+||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}\big)||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}

\displaystyle+c||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}^{1/2}||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle+c||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n-1})}^{1/2}||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h})}^{1/2}||{\mathbf{e}}_{\mathbf{u}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}^{1/2}||\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}||_{a_{h}},

for all
\mathbf{v}_{h}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}
, where
c>0
constant independent of
h
and
t
and
k_{g}\geq 2
.

Proof.

The proof follows identically to
[
33
, Lemma 7.14]
, where one uses integration by parts (
4.56
), (
4.16
), and the
W^{1,\infty}
-Ritz-Stokes bounds (
5.19
), along with the fact that
k_{g}\geq 2
, to obtain the desired estimate. This time, we also use the time-inequality bounds (
7.6
), were appropriate.
∎

Now, instead of
Lemma

7.8
, we prove the following auxiliary result.

Lemma 7.15

(Auxiliary result II)
.

Assume
\underline{k_{\lambda}=k_{u}-1}
and
k_{g}\geq 2
, and let the regularity
2
hold with time-step
\Delta t\leq ch
. Let
({\mathbf{u}},\{p,\lambda\})
be the solution of (
eNSWc
) and let
(\mathbf{u}_{h}^{k},\{p_{h}^{k},\lambda_{h}^{k}\})
,
k=1,...,n
be the discrete solutions of (
eNSWch
), with initial condition
\mathbf{u}_{h}^{0}=\mathcal{R}_{h}^{b}{\mathbf{u}}^{0}
. Then, the following estimate holds for
1\leq n\leq N

(7.69)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\partial^{\circ}_{h,\tau}\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{k},\partial^{\circ}_{h,\tau}\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{k})+||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\frac{h^{2m}}{\Delta t})\Big(\big(\Delta t\big)^{2}+h^{2m}\Big)+\epsilon\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{n}||\sigma_{\{p,\lambda\}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2},

where
m=min\{r_{u},k_{pr}+1,k_{\lambda}+1\}
,
r_{u}=min\{k_{u},k_{g}-1\}
, with positive constant
C=C(\epsilon)
depending on arbitrary
\epsilon>0
and the regularity
2
.

Proof.

To start with, recall (
7.54
) and the fact that
\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}=\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(\cdot,t_{n})+\Delta t\,\partial^{\circ}_{h,\tau}\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}
by (
6.8
) to see that

(7.70)

\displaystyle{\dfrac{1}{\Delta t}\Big({\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t^{n-1}))\Big)}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\partial^{\circ}_{h,\tau}\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\Delta t}\big[{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}),\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n})-{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\underline{\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t^{n-1}))\big].

Then testing the equation error equations (
EQc
) with
\mathbf{v}_{h}^{n}=\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n})\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n}
and
\{q_{h}^{n},\xi_{h}^{n}\}=\sigma_{\{p,\lambda\}}^{n}
, and using simple calculations similar to (
7.34
) to derive

(7.71)

\displaystyle a_{h}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\big(||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}^{2}-||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{2}\big)+\frac{1}{2}||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n})||_{a_{h}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\big(a_{h}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1})-a_{h}(\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}),\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}))\big),

along with the fact that
\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}\in{\mathbf{V}}_{h}^{n,div}
,
n=0,...,N
, thus
b_{h}^{L}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\{q_{h}^{n},\xi_{h}^{n}\})=b_{h}^{L}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\{\underline{q_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t_{n-1}),\underline{\xi_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t_{n-1})\})=0

\text{ for all }\{q_{h}^{n},\xi_{h}^{n}\}\in Q_{h}^{n}\times\Lambda_{h}^{n}
,
\{\underline{q_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t_{n-1}),\underline{\xi_{h}^{n}}(\cdot,t_{n-1})\}\in Q_{h}^{n-1}\times\Lambda_{h}^{n-1}
by (
6.16
) and therefore

\displaystyle b_{h}^{L}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}),\sigma_{\{p,\lambda\}}^{n})

\displaystyle=b_{h}^{L}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1},\underline{\sigma_{\{p,\lambda\}}^{n}}(t_{n-1}))-b_{h}^{L}(\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}),\sigma_{\{p,\lambda\}}^{n})

\displaystyle\leq c(\epsilon)\Delta t||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{2}+\epsilon\Delta t||\sigma_{\{p,\lambda\}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}\qquad\text{by \eqref{eq: time differences properties bilinear b ENS} and $\epsilon$-Weighted Young's Ineq.},

we derive, once we also apply the time inequality (
6.11
) to the last difference in (
7.71
) and (
6.9
) on the last difference in (
7.70
) together with Young’s inequality and the fact that
||\cdot||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}\leq||\cdot||_{a_{h}}
, the following

(7.72)

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\Delta t\,{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\partial^{\circ}_{h,\tau}\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\partial^{\circ}_{h,\tau}\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n})+\frac{1}{2}\big(||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}||_{a_{h}}-||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{2}\big)+\frac{1}{2}||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n})||_{a_{h}}^{2}\leq c(\epsilon)\Delta t||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}||_{a_{h}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\Big(|\sum_{i=1}^{3}\textsc{Err}_{i}^{C}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}))|+|\textsc{Err}_{1}^{I}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}))|+|\mathcal{C}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}))|

\displaystyle+|{\mathbf{m}}_{h}(\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n},\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n}))|\Big)+\frac{1}{6\Delta t}||\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n}-\underline{\sigma_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{n-1}}(t_{n})||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}+\epsilon\Delta t||\sigma_{\{p,\lambda\}}^{n}||_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{h}^{n})}^{2}.
